TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间随机控制中Q-learning的算子核心，在扩散设定下分析Bellman最优性目标的正则性与逼近复杂度。证明了Bellman更新将有界输入映射到各向异性正则类，并提出适应混合正则性的张量积DeepONet架构，给出了显式的逼近与资源界以及时间步长趋近于零时的刚度-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于值的强化学习中，Q-learning/DQN更新依赖于Bellman最优性目标，但连续状态-动作空间中该目标的正则性及其逼近复杂性尚缺乏严格的理论分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，为连续随机控制中的Q-learning提供算子层面的理论基础。

Method: 在均匀椭圆性和Hölder正则系数的扩散设定下，利用算子理论分析Bellman更新的正则性提升性质；证明Bellman算子将有界输入映射到状态变量各向异性光滑、动作变量仅Lipschitz依赖的混合正则类；基于此设计张量积DeepONet架构，并推导逼近误差与资源消耗的理论界。

Result: 证明了Bellman更新产生紧的迭代序列族，揭示了状态变量与动作变量在正则性上的不对称性；给出了显式的逼近误差界和资源复杂度界，并发现了时间步长δ→0时刚度与计算复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该理论对连续随机控制中Bellman目标的正则性和逼近问题做出了直接贡献，为理解Q-learning在连续空间中的行为提供了算子层面的基础。但本文未给出实际采样Q-learning（含探索、经验回放和随机梯度更新）的完整收敛性定理。

Abstract: We study the operator-theoretic core of Q-learning in continuous-time stochastic control with continuous states and actions. In value-based reinforcement learning, each Q-learning or DQN update is built from a Bellman optimality target; our analysis isolates this target in a diffusion setting and studies its regularity and approximation complexity. Under uniform ellipticity and Hölder-regular coefficients, we show that a Bellman update maps bounded inputs into an anisotropic regularity class, smoothing the state variable while leaving only Lipschitz dependence on the action variable. This yields a compact family of Bellman iterates and motivates a tensor-product DeepONet architecture adapted to the mixed regularity of the problem. We then derive explicit approximation and resource bounds, together with a stiffness--complexity trade-off as the time step $δ\to 0$. The resulting theory makes a direct contribution to Q-learning theory at the level of Bellman target regularity and approximation in continuous stochastic control. At the same time, we do not claim a full convergence theorem for practical sampled Q-learning with exploration, replay, and stochastic gradient updates.

</details>


### [183] [HawkesNest: A Multi-Axis Synthetic Benchmark for Spatiotemporal Pattern Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16863)
*Yahya Aalaila,Sumantrak Mukherjee,Gerrit Großmann,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HawkesNest是一个基于多元Hawkes过程的生成器对齐基准，用于可控的时空模式复杂度评估，定义了四个复杂度轴并关联确定性指标，能够对STPP模型进行诊断性压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STPP模型评估依赖不透明的真实世界数据集，其潜在生成结构未知，模型失败难以归因，缺乏可控的基准来系统诊断模型在不同复杂度维度下的表现。

Method: 基于多元Hawkes过程构建生成器对齐基准，定义时空纠缠、背景异质性、跨类型交互和领域拓扑四个复杂度轴，每个轴关联一个从数据生成机制计算出的确定性指标，在保持全局速率、稳定性和模拟预算固定的条件下变化这些轴。

Result: 验证了各复杂度指标在受控扫描下是单调且近似正交的；Hawkes族基线在联合异质性-纠缠复杂度下退化；AutoSTPP在时空纠缠单独增加时仍显脆弱。

Conclusion: HawkesNest提供了一个可控、可归因的评估框架，能够有效暴露STPP模型在不同复杂度维度下的性能退化，有助于推动时空点过程模型的诊断性测试与改进。

Abstract: Evaluation of spatiotemporal point process (STPP) models relies heavily on opaque real-world datasets, where latent generative structure is unknown and model failures are difficult to attribute. We introduce HawkesNest, a generator-aligned benchmark for controlled spatiotemporal pattern complexity built on a multivariate Hawkes backbone. HawkesNest defines four complexity axes: space--time entanglement, background heterogeneity, cross-type interaction, and domain topology. Each axis is associated with a deterministic index computed from the latent data-generating mechanism. By varying these axes while holding global rate, stability, and simulation budget fixed, HawkesNest enables diagnostic stress tests of STPP models under known structural difficulty. We verify that the indices are monotone and nearly orthogonal under controlled sweeps. We illustrate its use by showing that Hawkes-family baselines degrade under joint heterogeneity--entanglement complexity, even though they are structurally aligned with the Hawkes data-generating backbone. We further show that HawkesNest exposes neural-model sensitivity: AutoSTPP remains vulnerable under isolated increases in space--time entanglement. Code. Available at https://github.com/YahyaAalaila/HawkesNest

</details>


### [184] [Integrated Marketing Attribution: A Bayesian Framework for Privacy-Safe Granular Measurement Anchored in MMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16878)
*Meghana R. Bhat,Ankit Umare,Utsav Aggarwal,Richard Vecsler,Arunkumar Mani,Karthik Nair,Chandhu Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出集成营销归因（IMA）框架，通过将营销组合模型（MMM）与基于贝叶斯的渠道级归因模型相结合，在无需用户级追踪的情况下，从聚合数据中推导出粒度化的活动级效果，实现隐私安全且与MMM一致的归因分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零售营销测量方法中，MMM（隐私安全、适合渠道规划但粒度粗）与MTA（粒度细但隐私限制下可靠性下降）产生碎片化洞察，无法同时满足隐私安全与精细化活动优化的需求。

Method: 提出IMA框架，将MMM与渠道特定的贝叶斯归因模型结合，利用MMM提供的先验信息，从聚合数据推导活动级效果，实现隐私安全且与MMM保持一致的归因。

Result: IMA框架能够在隐私限制环境下提供粒度化的活动级归因洞察，同时保持与MMM在渠道规划层面的一致性。

Conclusion: IMA有效弥合了MMM与MTA之间的鸿沟，为零售营销测量提供了一种兼顾隐私安全与归因粒度的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Retail marketing measurement increasingly requires granular campaign-level insights without relying on user-level tracking. However, the two dominant approaches, Marketing Mix Modeling (MMM) and Multi-Touch Attribution (MTA), often produce fragmented insights. MMM is privacy-safe and robust for channel-level planning but is too coarse for campaign optimization, while MTA provides granular attribution but has become less reliable under increasing privacy restrictions. We propose Integrated Marketing Attribution (IMA), a unified framework that combines MMM with channel specific Bayesian attribution models to derive campaign-level effects from aggregated data. By leveraging MMM-informed priors, IMA delivers granular, privacy-safe attribution while preserving consistency with MMM.

</details>


### [185] [Upper Bounds on the Generalization Error of Deep Learning Models via Local Robustness and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16883)
*Abdul-Rauf Nuhu,Parham M. Kebria,Vahid Hemmati,Mahmoud N. Mahmoud,Edward Tunstel,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的泛化界，通过按输入空间子区域中稳定与不稳定样本数量缩放鲁棒性项，解决了现有鲁棒性泛化界在实际中常为空洞（上界远大于真实误差）的问题。在ImageNet上的实验表明，该方法保持非空洞且比现有方法更紧，更贴近实际性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于鲁棒性的泛化界在实际设置中常为空洞（上界远超真实误差），且鲁棒性项作为全局度量忽略了输入空间不同子区域的差异，导致实用性受限。

Method: 提出一种新的泛化界，按输入空间每个子区域内稳定样本和不稳定样本的数量对鲁棒性项进行缩放，同时纳入数据和模型相关因素。

Result: 在ImageNet上训练的多种鲁棒深度神经网络上，所提泛化界保持非空洞，且在现有方法中上界最紧，与经验性能高度一致。

Conclusion: 通过引入子区域级别的鲁棒性缩放，可以显著提升泛化界的紧致性和实用性，为实际鲁棒性评估提供更可靠的理论工具。

Abstract: Generalization is a critical property of data-driven models, particularly deep learning models deployed in safety-critical applications. Robustness-based generalization bounds have gained attention as a principled way to link robustness properties to generalization performance, often in a data-dependent manner. However, most existing bounds suffer from vacuousness in practical settings, yielding loose upper bounds that greatly exceed the actual error rates and limiting their usefulness for real-world evaluation. While this issue is often attributed to the uncertainty term, a substantial part of the problem originates from the robustness term itself, particularly for the 0-1 loss. Existing approaches typically treat the robustness term as a global measure, ignoring its variation across different sub-regions of the input space. In this work, we propose a generalization bound that addresses this limitation by scaling the robustness term according to the number of stable and unstable samples within each sub-region. Our bounds incorporate both data- and model-dependent factors while maintaining practical relevance (yielding tighter upper bounds on true error). Experiments on models trained on the ImageNet dataset show that our bounds remain consistently non-vacuous and achieve the tightest estimates among existing methods, closely aligning with empirical performance across a range of robust deep neural networks.

</details>


### [186] [Beyond Weights and Gradients: A Taxonomy of Federated Learning Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16891)
*Alvaro Javier Vargas Guerrero,Xinguang Wang,Quang Manh Doan,Guy Nagels*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中消息定义的不足，提出了包含效用与隐私的正式数学定义，构建了包含模型结构、统计摘要和数据条件表示的三类消息分类法，并基于202篇文献综述揭示了2021年后联邦消息多样化的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习的定义无法涵盖现代交换内容（如合成数据、联邦分析），存在定义缺口，亟需一个统一的形式化框架来系统描述联邦消息的多样性与权衡。

Method: 提出联邦消息的正式数学定义（兼顾效用与隐私）；构建三类消息分类法（模型结构、统计摘要、数据条件表示）；从计算开销、通信成本和隐私风险三个维度评估各组；并对202篇近期文献进行系统综述。

Result: 202篇文献综述表明，自2021年以来联邦消息范式发生了显著转变，从标准深度学习更新向更专业化的信息共享演进。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来研究提供了结构化路径，有助于针对不同硬件和安全需求优化联邦系统。

Abstract: Federated Learning is rapidly evolving beyond the exchange of traditional model weights and gradients, yet existing definitions fail to capture the full scope of modern payloads like synthetic data and federated analytics. This paper addresses the gap by proposing a formal mathematical definition of a federated message that accounts for both utility and privacy. We introduce a taxonomy that organizes these exchanges into three categories: model structures, statistical summaries, and data-conditioned representations. By evaluating these groups based on computational demands, communication costs, and privacy risks, we provide a clearer understanding of the trade-offs involved in decentralized training. Our review of 202 recent publications highlights a significant shift since 2021 toward diverse messaging paradigms, signaling a move away from standard deep learning updates toward more specialized information sharing. This framework provides a structured path for future research to optimize federated systems for varying hardware and security requirements.

</details>


### [187] [Fantastic Pretraining Optimizers and Where to Find Them II: Hyperball Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16899)
*Kaiyue Wen,Xingyu Dang,Kaifeng Lyu,Tengyu Ma,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hyperball是一种简单的优化器封装方法，通过将权重矩阵及其对应优化器更新的Frobenius范数设为固定常数，解决了Muon等基于矩阵的优化器在大规模模型训练中相比AdamW优势递减的问题，在Qwen3风格模型上实现20-30%的token等效加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等基于矩阵的优化器在语言模型预训练中相比AdamW有显著加速效果，但随着模型规模和数据的增长，在标准常数解耦权重衰减设置下，其优势逐渐缩小，需要解决这一扩展性问题。

Method: Hyperball是一种优化器封装方法，接收Adam或Muon等基础优化器，将权重矩阵及其对应的优化器更新的Frobenius范数固定为常数，替代标准的解耦权重衰减。

Result: 在Qwen3风格模型上（最高1.2B参数），Muon Hyperball相比权重衰减基线实现了20-30%的token等效加速；同时相比解耦权重衰减，改善了学习率在不同宽度和深度间的迁移性。

Conclusion: 权重衰减训练会导致一个仅依赖于训练超参数的均衡权重范数，通过该机制权重衰减决定了角学习率（即权重矩阵方向变化的速度），Hyperball通过固定范数来消除这一依赖性，从而在大规模场景下保持优化器优势。

Abstract: Matrix based optimizers such as Muon can substantially speed up language model pretraining, but their gains over AdamW are observed to shrink as model size and data scale grow when using standard constant decoupled weight decay. We propose Hyperball, a simple optimizer wrapper that addresses this issue. Given a base optimizer such as Adam or Muon, Hyperball sets the Frobenius norms of weight matrices and their corresponding optimizer updates to fixed constants. On Qwen3 style models up to 1.2B parameters, Muon Hyperball achieves 20--30% token equivalent speedup over weight decay baselines. Hyperball also improves learning rate transfer across widths and depths compared to decoupled weight decay. This method is motivated by prior theory showing that training with weight decay leads to an equilibrium weight norm that only depends on the training hyperparameters. Through this mechanism, the weight decay then decides the angular learning rate, i.e. how fast the direction of the weight matrix changes.

</details>


### [188] [Factorized Neural Operators Decompose Dynamic and Persistent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16900)
*Hao Tang,Yuechen Duan,Jiongyu Zhu,Zimeng Feng,Hao Li,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因子化神经算子（FaNO），将谱分解为等变动态响应和不变持久响应，分别捕捉快速瞬变动力学和持久的相干结构，从而在多个物理系统中提升预测精度、参数效率和跨尺度泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子依赖单一主导归纳偏置，将不同的物理响应耦合到共享表示中，难以刻画物理系统中快速演化动力学与持久结构并存的异构多尺度行为。

Method: 提出统一格林函数框架和因子化神经算子（FaNO），将谱表示分解为等变动态响应分支和不变持久响应分支，两个分支自发分工为快速瞬变动力学和相干持久结构。

Result: FaNO在长时自回归滚动、跨分辨率外推和物理状态转移下均保持一致的预测性能，在多个物理系统和领域上提升了预测精度、参数效率和跨尺度泛化能力。

Conclusion: 可扩展的物理建模应从单一归纳偏置转向因子化算子表示，以更好地反映物理系统的异构组织方式，加速机器学习在科学计算与发现中的可靠部署。

Abstract: Physical systems often exhibit heterogeneous mechanisms, where rapidly evolving dynamics coexist with persistent structures. Capturing such multiscale physical behavior remains challenging for existing neural operators, which typically rely on single dominant inductive bias and therefore couple distinct physical responses into a shared representation. We introduce the Unified Green's Function Framework across domains and propose the Factorized Neural Operators (FaNO), which decompose spectral representations into equivariant dynamic responses and invariant persistent responses, leading to better interpretability and generalization. Mechanistically, we show that the two operator branches spontaneously specialize into distinct physical roles that remain consistent across scales and domains: the equivariant branch captures rapidly varying transient dynamics, whereas the invariant branch extracts coherent persistent structures. This factorized mechanism of FaNO improves prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency and cross-scale generalization across physical systems and domains. In particular, it maintains consistent predictions under long-horizon autoregressive rollout, cross-resolution extrapolation and physical-regime shifts. These findings suggest that scalable physical modeling may benefit from moving beyond single-inductive-bias formulations toward factorized operator representations that better reflect the heterogeneous organization of physical systems, accelerating the reliable deployment of machine learning for scientific computing and discovery.

</details>


### [189] [Demystifying Variance in Circuit Discovery of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16920)
*Frank Zhengqing Wu,Francesco Tonin,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了电路发现方法EAP-IG存在的三种方差问题（重采样方差、改述方差、样本方差），提出改进方法CEAP并研究方差的根源，发现改述方差源于不同模板激活不同电路，样本方差主要由不忠度的定义缺陷而非电路缺陷导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的电路发现方法EAP-IG虽然在（不）忠度指标上表现良好，但存在显著的可变性（包括重采样方差、改述方差和样本方差），需要研究这些方差的根源并改进方法。

Method: 提出了CEAP方法（改进自EAP-IG，具有理论保证），通过对EAP-IG的改进来减少重采样方差；通过实验分析不同提示模板对激活电路的影响来研究改述方差；通过选择性贡献缩放机制来解释样本方差不忠度得分极差的现象。

Result: CEAP能显著减轻重采样方差；发现改述方差源于不同模板激活不同电路；稀疏性无法解决该问题；样本方差中的极差得分主要源于不忠度定义问题而非电路缺陷，选择性贡献缩放机制是导致极差得分的神经机制。

Conclusion: 找到一个能够解释和控制模型行为的全面电路可能具有挑战性，因为不同模板会激活不同电路，这意味着LLM本质上可能难以被操控；样本方差在很大程度上是良性的。

Abstract: Circuit discovery is a key technique in mechanistic interpretability to pinpoint the model components that are crucial for performing a given task. Although the current state-of-the-art method (EAP-IG) performs well on the metric of (un)faithfulness, it suffers from substantial variability. This includes resampling variance, where the circuit changes when we probe with a new batch of data from the same distribution; rephrasing variance, where the discovered circuit shifts when the prompts are rephrased; and sample-wise variance, where a circuit with low population unfaithfulness exhibits large fluctuations in unfaithfulness across individual samples.
  This paper studies the roots of these variances. We demonstrate that CEAP, our new circuit discovery method that improves upon EAP-IG with a theoretical guarantee, can substantially lessen resampling variance. We further show that rephrasing variance arises because prompts with different templates tend to activate different circuits in the model. This leads us to argue that it may be challenging to find a comprehensive circuit that explains and controls the model's behavior on a task, which can be expressed in countless templates, suggesting that LLMs may be inherently hard to steer. We show that sparsity, which has been claimed to form more compact and interpretable task circuits, fails to solve this problem. Regarding sample-wise variance, we argue that it is largely benign: extremely poor unfaithfulness scores often stem from how unfaithfulness is defined, rather than from defects in the measured circuits. We show that the magnitude of unfaithfulness is affected by selective contribution scaling, a neural mechanism that accounts for the extremely poor scores sometimes observed.

</details>


### [190] [A Unified Causal-Origin Taxonomy of Distributional Shifts in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16933)
*Ardianto Wibowo,Paulo E Santos,Amer Baghdadi,Matthew Stephenson,Karl Sammut,Jean-Philippe Diguet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一因果起源分类法，用于刻画强化学习中分布偏移的来源，将同分布/异分布泛化与非平稳设置联系起来，并引入评估框架来测量偏移影响与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习系统在运行条件变化时性能下降，且已有工作主要关注缓解策略而非分布偏移的因果起源，同时同分布/异分布泛化与非平稳设置之间的形式化关系尚不明确。

Method: 基于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）将交互过程分解为状态分布、观测过程、策略、奖励和转移动力学等结构组件，结合偏移时间边界，将经典数据集偏移原理从监督学习迁移至强化学习，构建统一分类法。

Result: 区分了内部（智能体驱动）和外部（环境驱动）分布偏移，进一步从偏移时间边界视角刻画显式、隐式和混合偏移，将同分布/异分布泛化和非平稳性统一为底层过程的结构化变化，并提出了性能退化和恢复指标的评估框架。

Conclusion: 通过将分布偏移扎根于强化学习的因果起源结构，该工作支持在分布偏移下进行系统化的鲁棒性分析。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) systems often degrade when operating conditions differ from those previously encountered, reflecting distributional shifts in the underlying data-generating process. Such shifts may occur between training and evaluation, as in In-Distribution (ID) and Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization, or within non-stationary settings where environment dynamics evolve over time. However, the formal relationship between these views remains unclear, and existing work mainly focuses on mitigation rather than the causal origin of shift within the agent-environment interaction. This work develops a unified causal-origin taxonomy that characterizes sources of distributional shift in RL and relates ID/OOD generalization to non-stationary settings. We transfer the classical dataset-shift principle from supervised learning to RL by reformulating distributional shift in terms of the generative interaction process. Using a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP), we decompose the interaction into structural components, including the state distribution, observation process, policy, reward, and transition dynamics, together with the shifted-time boundary. The proposed taxonomy distinguishes internal, agent-driven, and external, environment-driven, distributional shifts. The shifted-time boundary perspective further characterizes explicit, implicit, and hybrid shifts. This formulation unifies ID/OOD generalization and non-stationarity as structured changes in the underlying process. We also introduce an evaluation framework for measuring shift impact and adaptation through performance degradation and recovery metrics. By grounding distributional shift in the causal-origin structure of RL, this work supports systematic analysis of robustness under distributional shift.

</details>


### [191] [Scalable Circuit Learning for Interpreting Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16939)
*Naiyu Yin,Dennis Wei,Tian Gao,Amit Dhurandhar,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy,Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CircuitLasso，一种基于稀疏线性回归的可扩展电路学习方法，能以更低计算成本恢复与最先进干预方法结构精度相当的电路，并高效揭示SAE特征间的关系以提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始神经元具有多义性导致学习到的电路难以解释，而SAE特征虽然缓解了这一问题但高维特性使得现有基于干预的电路学习方法计算上不可行。

Method: 基于稀疏线性回归（Lasso）的电路学习方法CircuitLasso，通过回归方式学习LLM组件间稀疏电路，替代传统的干预式方法。

Result: 在基准数据上结构精度与最先进的干预方法相当，但计算成本大幅降低；能高效揭示SAE语义特征如何通过模型传播并影响预测。

Conclusion: CircuitLasso证明了基于回归的电路学习方法在可扩展性和可解释性上的优势，并可通过电路见解在领域泛化任务上以更低成本取得可比性能。

Abstract: A prominent research direction in mechanistic interpretability is learning sparse circuits over LLM components to reveal how they jointly produce model behavior. However, raw neurons are polysemantic, making learned circuits hard to interpret. Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features alleviate this, but their high dimensionality makes existing intervention-based circuit learning methods computationally prohibitive. We propose CircuitLasso, a scalable circuit-learning approach based on sparse linear regression. CircuitLasso recovers circuits whose structural accuracy matches that of state-of-the-art intervention-based methods on the benchmark data, at a fraction of the computational cost. For interpretability, CircuitLasso efficiently uncovers relationships among SAE features, showing how human-interpretable semantic features propagate through the model and influence its predictions. Finally, we validate the utility of our learned circuits by leveraging their insights to achieve comparable performance at substantially lower cost on a domain-generalization task.

</details>


### [192] [Phantoms and Disclosures: a Causal Framework for Auditing Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16952)
*Kareem Amin,Rudrajit Das,Alessandro Epasto,Adel Javanmard,Dennis Kraft,Mónica Ribero,Sergei Vassilvitskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需模型访问、无需插入金丝雀数据或训练参考模型的实证审计框架，通过区分真实披露和幻象披露，并结合统计假设检验来检测和解释生成式AI对私有训练数据的记忆与泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和LLM在生成高质量合成数据时可能记忆并泄露训练语料中的隐私信息，现有审计方法或需要模型访问、金丝雀插入或参考模型训练，计算成本高且适用性受限。

Method: 将输入数据划分为训练集和保留集；引入机制区分真实披露（系统直接复现用户信息）与幻象披露（偶然生成）；基于统计假设检验判断披露是否与零学习或特定差分隐私界限等严格隐私基线一致；仅需合成输出和保留控制集。

Result: 该框架可作为成员推断攻击，提供比先前基于数据的审计方法更紧的隐私泄露经验下界，计算资源需求比影子模型或金丝雀基线方法低数个数量级，且适用于任何合成数据生成机制。

Conclusion: 提出了一种模型无关、高效且可定制的隐私泄露实证审计框架，能够在无模型访问的条件下有效检测和解释合成数据生成过程中的隐私泄露问题。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative AI and Large Language Models (LLMs) has spurred interest in synthetic data as a privacy-preserving alternative to sensitive real-world datasets. However, generating high-utility synthetic data often carries the risk of memorizing and regurgitating private information from the training corpus. In this work, we present a customizable empirical auditing framework designed to detect and explain such data disclosures. Our framework introduces a mechanism to distinguish between "true disclosures"-where the system directly reproduces a user's information-and "phantom disclosures''-where the system incidentally generates a user's data. By partitioning input data into training and holdout sets and applying rigorous statistical hypothesis testing, we determine if observed disclosures are consistent with strict privacy baselines, such as zero-learning or specific Differential Privacy (DP) bounds. Crucially, this approach requires no model access, no canary insertion, and no reference model training -only the synthetic output and a held-out control set. We demonstrate that this framework effectively functions as a membership inference attack, providing empirical lower bounds on privacy leakage that are tighter than prior data-based auditing methods. Our approach is model-agnostic, applies to any synthetic data generation mechanism, and requires orders of magnitude fewer computational resources than shadow-model or canary-based alternatives.

</details>


### [193] [Beyond the Smile: A Hybrid Convolutional VAE for Crypto Volatility Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16961)
*Sadanand Singh,Allam Reddy,Manan Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合卷积变分自编码器与二次微笑曲线重拟合的混合预测器，用于加密货币隐含波动率曲面的补全与预测。在BTC和ETH的期权数据上，该混合模型在50%掩码率下将预测误差从7.00 vol点降至0.83 vol点，降幅达八倍，且无额外推理成本。模型在结构性缺失模式下（如整条期限的缺失）仍保持低误差（1.5-1.9 vol点），而传统微笑重拟合法误差高达9.6-13.1 vol点。联合训练BTC和ETH使模型性能提升9-27%，表明两者共享大量波动率曲面流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币期权市场的隐含波动率曲面存在大量缺失数据和不规则结构，传统参数化微笑重拟合方法在高缺失率下表现不佳，亟需一种能够鲁棒地完成曲面补全且保持无套利性质的生成模型。

Method: 使用卷积变分自编码器学习6×7 tenor-delta网格上的波动率曲面流形，再通过确定性每期限路由规则与二次微笑曲线重拟合结合构成混合预测器。模型联合训练于BTC和ETH数据，并设计了日历套利和无蝶式套利约束。

Result: 混合模型在50%掩码率下RMSE为0.83 vol点（vs 微笑重拟合法7.00），结构性缺失下误差1.5-1.9 vs 9.6-13.1 vol点；联合训练提升9-27%；模型成功识别出ETF预期行情和8月闪崩等异常时期。

Conclusion: 生成式模型是加密货币波动率曲面补全任务中唯一可行的预测器，混合框架在保持无套利性质的同时大幅提升预测精度，且跨币种联合训练揭示了BTC和ETH波动率流形的共享结构。

Abstract: We present a convolutional variational autoencoder for cryptocurrency implied-volatility surfaces, together with a deployable predictor that combines it with a quadratic smile re-fit through a deterministic per-tenor routing rule. Trained on 6,034 fully-filled hourly Binance Options surfaces of BTC and ETH spanning May-October 2023 and parameterised on a common $6 \times 7$ tenor-delta grid, the model attains a hidden-cell surface-completion RMSE in the 0.94-1.56 vol-point range across both markets and mask rates 10-50%. The hybrid predictor attains 0.83 vol points at 50% masking against 7.00 for the smile re-fit alone, an eightfold reduction obtained at no additional inference cost. Under structurally-correlated hole patterns that emulate the withdrawal of an entire tenor of strikes, the smile re-fit incurs 9.6-13.1 vol points of error while the learned model remains at 1.5-1.9, isolating a regime in which the generative model is the only viable predictor. Joint training on BTC and ETH improves the in-distribution model on both markets by 9-27% relative to the better-performing single-symbol counterpart, indicating a substantially shared vol-surface manifold across the two largest cryptocurrencies over the observation window. The hybrid is calendar- and butterfly-arbitrage-free at the listed strikes, a property that the parametric smile re-fit alone fails at high mask rates. The per-snapshot reconstruction error of the trained model flags the late-October ETF-anticipation rally and the August $17$, $2023$ flash crash as elevated-error periods without supervision. All training and evaluation infrastructure is released to support reproducible follow-on work.

</details>


### [194] [Scalable Pairwise Kernel Learning with Stochastic Vec Trick](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16979)
*Napsu Karmitsa,Tapio Pahikkala,Antti Airola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPaiK，一种可扩展的核学习方法，用于成对学习场景。通过引入随机广义vec技巧（sGVT），该方法在保持核方法表达力的同时大幅降低了计算和内存需求，使基于核的成对学习能够应用于此前无法处理的大规模数据集。在七个真实药物-靶标亲和力数据集上的评估显示其性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对学习在药物-靶标亲和力预测等领域至关重要，但传统核方法在处理大规模成对数据时面临计算和内存瓶颈，亟需一种可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出SPaiK方法，核心创新是随机广义vec技巧（sGVT），这是稀疏Kronecker乘积乘法算法的随机扩展，能够高效实现大规模成对核的训练。

Result: 在七个真实药物-靶标亲和力数据集上，SPaiK的性能优于当前最先进的成对学习方法。

Conclusion: SPaiK通过sGVT技巧成功将核方法的强大表达力扩展到大规模成对学习场景，解决了传统方法无法处理的数据规模问题。

Abstract: Pairwise learning is a specialized form of supervised learning that focuses on predicting outcomes for pairs of objects. In this work, we introduce SPaiK, a new scalable kernel learning method tailored for pairwise settings. Our approach preserves the expressive power of kernel methods while substantially reducing computational and memory requirements. The key innovation is the stochastic generalized vec trick (sGVT), a stochastic extension of the sparse Kronecker product multiplication algorithm, which enables efficient large-scale training with pairwise kernels. By incorporating sGVT, SPaiK makes it possible to apply kernel-based pairwise learning to datasets of a size previously out of reach. We evaluate the performance of SPaiK on seven real-world drug-target affinity datasets and compare the results with state-of-the-art methods in pairwise learning.

</details>


### [195] [Analytic Torsion and Spectral Gap Capture Persistent-Laplacian Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16990)
*Jernej Grlj,Aaron D. Lauda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对持久拉普拉斯算子因高维度和跨过滤尺度的“变长”问题难以直接用于学习任务，提出了一种紧凑的谱表示方法，将持久拉普拉斯算子蒸馏为三个数学上严格的不变量：贝蒂数、谱间隙和解析挠率。在MNIST、QM-3D和SKEMPI WT等基准数据集上的实验表明，该降维特征空间能够捕捉全谱的核心预测信号，有时甚至表现更优，同时显著降低计算开销并避免高频特征值引入的噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久拉普拉斯算子（PL）比持久同调能提供更丰富的几何数据表示，但将其全特征谱用于学习任务时面临高维度和不同过滤尺度下特征长度不一（“变长”问题）的挑战，亟需一种紧凑、定长的谱表示方法。

Method: 提出将持久拉普拉斯算子蒸馏为三个数学不变量：贝蒂数（Betti numbers）、谱间隙（spectral gap）和解析挠率（analytic torsion），构建固定长度的紧凑谱表示特征空间。

Result: 在MNIST、QM-3D和SKEMPI WT基准数据集上，该降维特征空间能捕捉全谱的预测信号，部分情况下性能超越全谱，同时显著降低计算开销并规避高频特征值噪声。

Conclusion: 这三个不变量提供了一种基于原理的、固定长度的接口，连接了谱几何与拓扑学习，是高效且有效的持久拉普拉斯替代方案。

Abstract: While persistent Laplacians (PL) offer a richer geometric representation of data than persistent homology, utilizing their full eigenspectrum for learning tasks is often hampered by high dimensionality and the ``varying length'' problem across different filtration scales. We propose a compact spectral representation that distills the persistent Laplacian into three mathematically grounded invariants: Betti numbers, the spectral gap, and analytic torsion. Across benchmark datasets including MNIST, QM-3D, and SKEMPI WT, we demonstrate that this reduced feature space captures the essential predictive signal of the full spectrum, and in some cases outperforms it, while significantly reducing computational overhead and preventing the noise introduced by higher-frequency eigenvalues. Our results suggest that these invariants provide a principled, fixed-length interface between spectral geometry and topological learning.

</details>


### [196] [From Tokens to Policy: Causal and Interpretable Heterogeneous Treatment Effects Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17010)
*Riccardo Cadei,Frank Otchere,Nyasha Tirivayi,Gustavo Angeles Tagliaferro,Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEXIS方法，通过多模态预处理表征和马尔可夫毯发现，在可控实验中识别异质性处理效应，避免混杂偏差，并在非洲两个扶贫项目中验证了其可解释性和政策优化价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HTE识别方法在表达性与可解释性之间权衡，但未测量的异质性驱动因素会导致虚假的HTE表征，缺乏因果解释力。本文旨在利用多模态、多视角预处理测量和可扩展表征，实现可解释的因果HTE识别。

Method: 将HTE识别重新定义为在充足且对齐的预处理表征上的马尔可夫毯发现问题，提出Neural Exposure Interaction Search (NEXIS)迭代算法，具有可证明的一致性选择保证。

Result: 在非洲两个反贫困项目中，通过引入卫星影像捕捉未测量的环境效应修饰因子，NEXIS发现了新颖、可解释且可指导政策优化的异质性处理效应。

Conclusion: 结合多模态预处理数据和NEXIS方法，可以在可控实验中实现具有因果含义的可解释HTE识别，为政策迭代提供规范性指导。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Treatment Effect (HTE) identification is crucial to explain the impact of an intervention and optimize our policies accordingly. Existing approaches trade expressivity for interpretability, but, if some active heterogeneity drivers are unmeasured, methods at both ends of this spectrum allow for spurious HTE characterization with no causal reading. In this work, we focus on controlled experiments and argue that an oracle HTE causal characterization via the latent interactors is now within reach, thanks to (i) more extensive pre-treatment measurements, i.e., multi-modal and multi-view, and (ii) scalable representations with minimal human supervision. We then re-frame HTE identification as a Markov-blanket discovery problem on a sufficient and aligned pre-treatment representation, and introduce Neural EXposure Interaction Search (NEXIS), an iterative procedure with provable and empirically validated consistent selection. We deploy NEXIS on two anti-poverty programs in Africa, augmenting each with satellite imagery capturing previously unmeasured environmental effect modifiers, leading to novel, interpretable and prescriptive guidelines to optimize the programs' next iterations.

</details>


### [197] [Filtered Conformal Ellipsoids for Graph-Native Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17014)
*Yannick Limmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于滤子共形椭球（filtered conformal ellipsoids）的多变量时间序列联合预测集构建方法，通过状态空间滤波器和分裂共形校准结合，在控制单一事件的同时适应跨坐标依赖，并提供了在依赖数据下的理论覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列的联合预测集需要控制单一事件（而非逐坐标）并适应跨坐标依赖性；现有方法难以同时满足这两个要求。滤波器的预测协方差可提供形状信息，但依赖高斯尾部概率的覆盖假设不稳健；共形预测虽在独立同分布数据下有效，但滤波后的得分具有依赖性且递归滤波器在隐状态空间中的收缩性难以保证。

Method: 采用冻结状态空间滤波器产生一步预测均值和协方差，基于马氏距离得分进行分裂共形校准以确定椭球半径；通过观测预测律商（observable predictive-law quotient）分析递归滤波器的收缩性；结合阈值-自协方差包络在依赖数据下推导切比雪夫型近似覆盖界，并在几何混合浓度假设下得到更紧的伯恩斯坦型界。

Result: 在METRLA-20和PEMSBAY-50中等规模图原生交通基准上，学习到的滤波器产生的目标椭球比静态协方差和无滤波器基线更紧；在全图规模和非图原生数据集上，因子模型和Copula基线表现更强。

Conclusion: 滤子共形椭球在理论上提供了依赖数据下的近似覆盖保证，并在实际交通数据上展现了优于非滤波基线的性能；但在大规模和非图原生场景下仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Joint prediction sets for multivariate time series should control a single event while adapting to cross-coordinate dependence. We study filtered conformal ellipsoids: a frozen state-space filter emits a one-step predictive mean and covariance, and split-conformal calibration is applied to the resulting Mahalanobis scores. The filter is used to choose the ellipsoid shape; conformal calibration chooses the scalar radius, so the construction benefits from a learned predictive covariance without relying on Gaussian tail probabilities for coverage. The main difficulty is that filtered scores are dependent and learned recurrent filters need not contract in their raw hidden state; we therefore analyse contraction in an observable predictive-law quotient that identifies hidden states producing the same future sequence of emitted Gaussian laws. Under a stable Bayes Gaussian-projection filter, covariance bounds, and a finite-horizon observability Fisher condition, small excess Gaussian negative log-likelihood implies contraction of the learned emitted laws. Combined with a threshold-autocovariance envelope this yields a Chebyshev-type approximate coverage bound for filtered split-conformal prediction under dependence; a sharper Bernstein-type bound requires an additional geometric-mixing concentration assumption. Under Gaussian oracle realisability we also obtain a near-oracle log-volume comparison within the class of conditionally valid Gaussian ellipsoid rules. We instantiate the framework with a GCN-GRU filter with diagonal-plus-low-rank covariance. On moderate-size graph-native traffic benchmarks (METRLA-$20$ and PEMSBAY-$50$), the learned filter gives sharper at-target ellipsoids than static-covariance and non-filter baselines; at full-graph scale and on non-graph-native datasets, factor and copula baselines can be stronger.

</details>


### [198] [ExpRL: Exploratory RL for LLM Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17024)
*Violet Xiang,Amrith Setlur,Chase Blagden,Nick Haber,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpRL方法，利用人类编写的问答数据作为奖励脚手架（而非模仿目标），通过LLM裁判对策略生成的推理轨迹进行过程级或结果级密集奖励评估，从而在强化学习预训练阶段更有效地提升LLM推理能力。实验表明ExpRL在数学推理任务上优于SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏，并为后续稀疏奖励RL提供更好的初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励强化学习在LLM推理中效果依赖基础模型的覆盖范围，现有方法通过中训练人工标注的推理轨迹引入基元技能（如分解、验证、自纠正），但这种策略需要手动指定学习内容，且不清楚组合这些基元技能能否解决更复杂的问题。因此需要更自动化的中训练方法。

Method: ExpRL方法将参考解答作为奖励脚手架（reward scaffolds），隐藏参考解答不让策略模型看到，仅用于构建问题特定的评分标准。策略模型从原始问题提示中采样，LLM裁判将采样的推理轨迹与参考解答进行比较，分配过程级或结果级的密集奖励。这使得ExpRL能够强化部分进展、有用的中间化简和富有成效的推理行为。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学推理任务上，ExpRL在RL预训练方面优于SFT、稀疏奖励GRPO和自蒸馏，并为后续的稀疏奖励RL提供更好的初始化。跨领域混合实验表明ExpRL可以扩展到数学以外的领域。

Conclusion: 利用人类问答数据作为奖励脚手架进行基于RL的中训练是一种更自动化的方法，能够有效提升LLM的推理能力，克服稀疏奖励的局限性，并具有良好的泛化潜力。

Abstract: Sparse reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard tool for improving LLM reasoning, but its success depends critically on the coverage present in the base model. In practice, models are often primed for RL through \emph{mid-training} on curated reasoning traces that teach useful primitive skills such as decomposition, verification, or self-correction. Although effective, this strategy requires manually specifying what the model should learn, and it remains unclear whether such primitive coverage is enough for much harder problems, which require combining these skills into broader solution strategies. We study a more automated approach: \emph{RL-based mid-training} using large corpora of human-written question-answer data. Rather than treating reference solutions as targets to imitate, our method, ExpRL, uses them as \emph{reward scaffolds}: references are hidden from the policy and used only to construct problem-specific grading rubrics for judging on-policy reasoning traces. The policy samples from the original problem prompt, while an LLM judge compares the sampled reasoning trace against the reference solution and assigns outcome-level or process-level dense rewards. This lets ExpRL reinforce partial progress, useful intermediate reductions, and productive reasoning behaviors that sparse final-answer rewards often fail to upweight. On challenging math reasoning tasks, ExpRL yields stronger RL priming than SFT, sparse-reward GRPO, and self-distillation, and provides a better initialization for subsequent sparse-reward RL. Additional mixed-domain experiments further suggest that ExpRL can extend beyond the original math-only setting.

</details>


### [199] [HAMON: Passive Optical Sequence Mixing for Long-Horizon Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17028)
*Alper Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HAMON是一种被动衍射光学预测核心，通过将历史值编码到光学孔径上，利用级联可训练相位掩模和自由空间衍射直接在输出场中生成预测，无需可训练的数字序列混合层。在标准时间序列基准测试中，HAMON在ETTm2数据集上全面优于最强数字基线，在ETTh2上除最长视界外均更优，MSE降低高达14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性模型和频域模型在长程时间序列预测中仍具竞争力，且近期证据表明标准预测基准可能不需要Transformer在其他领域强大的密集叠加表示。这引发了一个底层问题：如果核心预测算子通常是低复杂度和近似线性的，是否必须通过可学习的数字时间混合来实现？

Method: 将历史值编码到光学孔径上，未来位置保持暗场，通过级联可训练相位掩模和自由空间衍射直接在输出场中形成预测。推理时仅需一次被动光学传播，无需可训练的数字序列混合层。

Result: 在ETTm2上所有视界均优于最强数字基线，在ETTh2上除最长视界外均更优，MSE改善高达14%；在Weather数据集上具有竞争力；在Traffic和Electricity等高通道数数据集上落后于最强基线。

Conclusion: HAMON定义了光学硬件和被动物理序列混合的具体目标，消融实验和交叉模拟器验证表明预测来自承载数据的光场而非数字预测头。

Abstract: Simple linear and frequency-domain models remain surprisingly competitive in long-horizon time-series forecasting, and recent mechanistic evidence suggests that standard forecasting benchmarks may not require the dense superposed representations that make transformers powerful in other domains. This raises a substrate-level question: if the core forecasting operator is often low-complexity and approximately linear, does it need to be implemented as learned digital temporal mixing? We introduce HAMON, a passive diffractive optical forecasting core in which historical values are encoded onto an optical aperture, future positions are left dark, and cascaded trainable phase masks with free-space diffraction shape the forecast directly in the output field. At inference, prediction is performed by a single passive optical propagation pass with no trainable digital sequence-mixing layer.
  Across standard benchmarks, HAMON outperforms the strongest digital baselines considered on ETTm2 at all horizons and on ETTh2 at all but the longest horizon, improving MSE by up to 14\% and doing so consistently across horizons rather than at isolated points. It is competitive on Weather and trails the strongest baselines on the remaining ETT settings and on the high-channel-count Traffic and Electricity datasets. Phase encoding, intensity-compatible readout, and phase-scrambling ablations, together with a TorchOptics cross-simulator check, indicate that the forecasts arise from the data-bearing optical field rather than from a digital forecasting head. Because the passive core uses standard Fourier optics, HAMON defines a concrete target for optical hardware and for passive physical sequence mixing.

</details>


### [200] [Your Privacy My Cloak: Backdoor Attacks on Differentially Private Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17035)
*Xiaolin Li,Ning Wang,Ninghui Li,Wenhai Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了差分隐私（DP）能天然增强联邦学习（FL）对后门攻击鲁棒性的普遍假设。研究发现DP会掩盖恶意更新的统计特征，使现有防御失效。基于此，作者提出RING攻击，通过协作构造对抗性扰动，在聚合时重建强后门信号同时规避异常检测。在四个数据集和六种防御下，RING在中等隐私预算下达到90.3%的平均攻击成功率，比基线策略提升高达26.08倍。评估表明，缓解该威胁会带来显著的效用权衡，暴露了DP-FL部署中的根本安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"差分隐私天然增强联邦学习对后门攻击鲁棒性"这一被广泛接受的假设，揭示DP与后门攻击之间存在的根本性张力——即遵守DP规则会无意中掩盖恶意更新的统计特征，导致现有防御失效。

Method: 提出RING攻击方法，通过协作构造对抗性扰动，使受损客户端在聚合过程中重建强后门信号，同时利用DP的掩蔽效应规避异常检测。RING作为与底层后门技术无关的扰动层，可广泛适用并可与现有攻击组合。

Result: 在四个图像和文本数据集（非独立同分布设置）下，针对六种最先进防御方法，RING在中等隐私预算下达到90.3%的平均攻击成功率，较基线策略提升高达26.08倍。

Conclusion: 缓解RING这类攻击会带来显著的效用权衡，揭示了差分隐私联邦学习部署中存在的根本性安全漏洞。

Abstract: Prior research suggests that differential privacy (DP) inherently enhances the robustness of federated learning (FL) against backdoor attacks. In this paper, we challenge this assumption. Through an empirical analysis of two baseline attack strategies, we uncover a fundamental tension in DP-FL: while bypassing DP allows state-of-the-art defenses to detect and filter malicious updates, complying with DP inadvertently masks their distinguishing statistical characteristics. Consequently, existing defenses become ineffective as DP reduces the raw backdoor signal. Building on this masking effect, we propose RING, a novel attack that explicitly exploits DP to conceal malicious contributions while maximizing attack impact. By collaboratively crafting adversarial perturbations, compromised clients reconstruct a strong backdoor signal during aggregation without triggering anomaly detection. RING operates as a perturbation layer that is agnostic to the underlying backdoor technique, making it broadly applicable and composable with existing attacks -- a property that significantly amplifies the threat it poses to DP-FL. Extensive evaluations across four image and text datasets under non-iid distributions show that RING achieves an average attack success rate of 90.3% against six state-of-the-art defenses under a moderate privacy budget, an improvement of up to 26.08x over baseline strategies. Finally, we evaluate potential countermeasures and find that mitigating this threat incurs significant utility trade-offs, exposing a fundamental security gap in the deployment of differentially private FL.

</details>


### [201] [Exact Posterior Score Estimation for Solving Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17048)
*Abbas Mammadov,Ozgur Kara,Kaan Oktay,Iskander Azangulov,Adil Kaan Akan,Hyungjin Chung,James Matthew Rehg,Yee Whye Teh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Exact Posterior Score (EPS)方法，通过推导线性高斯逆问题下精确后验得分的闭式解，将后验采样转化为特定算子依赖的平移下的去噪问题，在保持预训练去噪器输入/输出结构的同时实现高效后验采样，在FFHQ和ImageNet上的五个线性逆问题中优于现有方法，且推理时无需似然梯度或投影，计算量比梯度方法少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流模型通过在数据上训练去噪器来学习先验分布，但求解线性逆问题时需要从后验分布中采样，而预训练模型提供的是无条件得分而非后验得分。现有方法要么在固定预训练去噪器上施加近似的测量匹配修正，要么训练条件恢复模型但放弃了去噪结构。需要一种既能保持去噪结构又能精确计算后验得分的方法。

Method: 推导了线性高斯逆问题在一般高斯插值下的精确后验得分闭式解，表明后验采样可转化为在算子依赖的平移点下、在异方差噪声协方差下的去噪问题。基于这一恒等式设计了EPS训练目标，保持标准预训练的去噪输入/输出结构，可从零训练或从预训练去噪器微调。推理时使用与骨干网络相同的采样器，无需似然梯度或投影操作。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet上的五个线性逆问题（包括超分辨率、去模糊、修复等）中，EPS在保真度、感知质量和分布度量上均优于训练无关和训练基线方法，且所需的去噪器评估次数比基于梯度的后验采样器少约一个数量级。

Conclusion: EPS方法通过理论推导将后验采样精确转化为去噪问题，既保持了预训练去噪器的结构优势，又实现了高效、精确的后验采样，为线性逆问题提供了一种新的训练范式。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based models learn powerful data priors by training a denoiser to reverse Gaussian corruption. To use this prior to solve a linear inverse problem, one needs to sample from the posterior, but the score that the prior provides is the unconditional score, not the posterior score. Existing methods either steer a fixed pretrained denoiser with approximate measurement-matching corrections, or train a conditional restoration model that abandons the denoising structure of the prior. We derive the exact posterior score in closed form for linear Gaussian inverse problems under general Gaussian interpolants, and show that posterior sampling reduces to a denoising problem at an operator-dependent shifted pivot under an anisotropic noise covariance. We turn this identity into Exact Posterior Score (EPS), a denoising training objective that preserves the input/output structure of standard pretraining and can therefore be trained from scratch or fine-tuned from a pretrained denoiser. At inference, EPS uses the same sampler as the underlying backbone, with no likelihood gradients or projections. We evaluate EPS on five linear inverse problems across FFHQ and ImageNet, where it outperforms training-free and training-based baselines on fidelity, perceptual, and distributional metrics, while using roughly an order of magnitude fewer denoiser evaluations than gradient-based posterior samplers.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Poster: EdgeCitadel -- Hybrid NATS-MQTT Orchestration for Edge Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14710)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Yefan Zhang,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EdgeCitadel是一个边缘多智能体编排平台，基于NATS 2.10服务器和MQTT适配器构建，支持异构智能体通信、JetStream持久化、直接对等委派和被动聚合器，已在ARM64、x64和Android设备上完成跨设备测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI智能体（家庭服务器、IoT集线器、笔记本电脑、手机）的协调栈仍沿用云端风格的传输方式或依赖中央中继，缺乏为边缘环境量身定制的轻量级、去中心化编排方案。

Method: 基于NATS 2.10服务器及其内置MQTT适配器，设计了混合架构：MQTT实现异构智能体连接，JetStream提供后端服务的持久化和重放，共享主题命名空间支持直接对等委派，被动聚合器在不位于传输路径上的情况下可视化并存储流量。

Result: 成功从MQTT中继原型迁移到混合架构，并在包含ARM64、x64和Android客户端的跨设备测试平台上完成了验证。

Conclusion: EdgeCitadel展示了边缘多智能体编排的可行方案，通过单一NATS服务器实现轻量化、去中心化的边缘协调，无需云端或中央中继。

Abstract: Edge-resident AI agents increasingly span home servers, IoT hubs, laptops, and phones, yet their coordination stacks still assume cloud-style transports or a central relay. We present EdgeCitadel, an edge multi-agent orchestration platform built around a single NATS 2.10 server with the built-in MQTT adapter. The design combines MQTT connectivity for heterogeneous agents, JetStream-backed persistence and replay for backend services, direct peer delegation over a shared subject namespace, and a passive aggregator that visualizes and stores traffic without sitting on the delivery path. Our poster highlights the migration from MQTT relay prototypes (common in IoT communication) to the current hybrid architecture and demonstrates a working cross-device testbed spanning ARM64, x64, and Android clients.

</details>


### [203] [SWARM-LLM: Collaborative Inference for Edge-based Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14711)
*Mostafa Dahshan,Quazi Mamun,Tanmoy Debnath*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SWARM-LLM 提出了一种路由与协作层，协调多个边缘端小型语言模型（SLM）与可选的云端基础模型（FM），通过不确定性估计和安全信号决定查询是在本地回答、协作回答还是调用云端模型，在性能、延迟和成本之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）部署在集中式云基础设施上，存在带宽、延迟和隐私成本高的问题；而小型语言模型（SLM）可在边缘设备运行但能力有限。因此需要一种混合方案来兼顾性能与隐私。

Method: 提出 SWARM-LLM 路由与协作层，利用轻量级不确定性估计和安全信号，为每个查询动态决策三种路径：本地 SLM 单独回答、多个 SLM 协作回答、或调用云端 FM。

Result: 在包含简单、困难和安全导向查询的受控工作负载上，SWARM-LLM 相比纯边缘部署显著提升了困难问题的性能，同时将云端调用限制在约四分之一的查询中。

Conclusion: SWARM-LLM 展示了在隐私敏感的边缘部署中，准确率、延迟和成本之间的实用权衡，实现了边缘 SLM 与云端 FM 的有效协同。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide strong performance across a wide range of tasks but are typically hosted on centralised cloud infrastructure, incurring significant bandwidth, latency, and privacy costs. In contrast, small language models (SLMs) can run on edge devices but have limited capability and robustness. This paper introduces SWARM-LLM, a routing and collaboration layer that coordinates a small swarm of edge-hosted SLMs with an optional cloud foundation model (FM). SWARM-LLM decides, for each query, whether to answer locally, collaborate with peer SLMs, or "summon" a cloud FM, using lightweight uncertainty estimates and safety signals. We implement a working prototype on commodity hardware with three heterogeneous SLMs and a 70B-parameter cloud FM accessed via API, and evaluate it on a controlled study workload of easy, hard, and safety-oriented queries. Our results show that SWARM-LLM substantially improves performance on hard questions compared to an edge-only deployment, while limiting cloud usage to roughly one quarter of queries, illustrating a practical trade-off between accuracy, latency, and cost for privacy-conscious edge deployments. The implementation code is available at the GitHub repository https://github.com/mdahshan/swarm_llm.

</details>


### [204] [Secure and Low-Latency IoT Analytics Using an Edge-Based Streaming Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14712)
*Atul,Varun Shukla,Vivek Shukla,Mehul Kumar Das*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EdgeStream是一个轻量级的边缘计算框架，用于物联网环境中的实时流数据分析。它通过将处理任务卸载到边缘节点，结合MQTT通信和分布式异常检测，相比纯云架构能降低延迟、减少带宽消耗，实验显示性能提升最高达92.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统云中心化架构因网络延迟和高数据传输开销，无法满足物联网大规模连续数据流的低延迟和带宽效率需求。

Method: 提出EdgeStream框架，集成边缘节点进行本地处理，云后端负责协调与存储，采用MQTT协议通信，结合分布式处理与异常检测机制，并建立了延迟、吞吐量和带宽的分析模型。

Result: 实验结果显示，相比纯云系统，EdgeStream在性能上提升高达92.8%。

Conclusion: EdgeStream所提出的边缘计算框架能够有效满足物联网实时流分析对低延迟和带宽效率的需求。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Internet of Things (IoT) devices has led to large-scale continuous data streams that require realtime processing. Traditional cloud-centric architectures fail to meet low-latency and bandwidth efficiency requirements due to network delays and high data transmission overhead. This paper proposes EdgeStream, a lightweight edge-based framework for real-time streaming analytics in IoT environments. The system integrates edge nodes for local processing with a cloud backend for coordination and storage, using MQTT-based communication and distributed processing with anomaly detection. Analytical models for latency, throughput, and bandwidth are developed to evaluate performance. Experimental results, compared to cloudbased systems, show up to 92.8

</details>


### [205] [Evaluating Gemma4 Models as AI Teaching Assistants for Introductory Parallel Programming: A DataRaceBench Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14881)
*Sabbir Hussain Meraj,Riham Chowdhury,Shimul Debnath,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了两种Gemma4开源模型（Gemma4-E4B和Gemma4-31B）在OpenMP程序数据竞争检测、解释和修复方面的能力。结果显示，较大的Gemma4-31B模型在解释（100/104）和修复（98/104）方面表现优异，接近完全覆盖基准测试集。有趣的是，额外上下文提示（如ThreadSanitizer报告）并未持续提升修复效果，有时甚至降低了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生由于并发执行的非确定性和共享内存语义的复杂性，在调试数据竞争时面临重大挑战。尽管大语言模型有潜力作为AI助教，但低成本开源模型在并行调试方面的能力尚不明确。

Method: 在DataRaceBench基准测试套件上，评估Gemma4-E4B和Gemma4-31B两种开源模型识别、解释和修复OpenMP程序中数据竞争的能力，并探讨额外上下文提示（TSan报告和模型生成的解释）对修复质量的影响。

Result: Gemma4-E4B正确解释了82/104个竞态程序并成功修复73个；Gemma4-31B实现了100个正确解释和98个成功修复。额外上下文并未持续提升修复效果，有时反而降低性能。

Conclusion: 开源LLM可以为学生的自我调试提供有价值的支持，其中较大的模型能够近乎完全覆盖基准测试集。

Abstract: Debugging data races is a major challenge for students learning parallel programming due to the non-deterministic nature of concurrent execution and the complexity of shared-memory semantics. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest that they could serve as AI teaching assistants, but the capabilities of lower-cost open-weight models for parallel debugging remain unclear. In this paper, we evaluate two Gemma4 open-weight models, Gemma4-E4B and Gemma4-31B, on their ability to identify, explain, and repair data races in OpenMP programs from the DataRaceBench benchmark suite. We also investigate whether contextual hints, including ThreadSanitizer (TSan) reports and model-generated explanations, improve repair quality. Our results show that Gemma4-E4B correctly explained 82 of 104 race-condition programs and successfully repaired 73, while Gemma4-31B achieved 100 correct explanations and 98 successful repairs. Surprisingly, additional context did not consistently improve repair effectiveness and sometimes reduced performance. These findings suggest that open-weight LLMs can provide valuable support for student self-debugging, with larger models offering near-complete coverage of the benchmark suite.

</details>


### [206] [NEURON-Fabric: CXL-Side Low-Bit Gradient Aggregation for Distributed Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15045)
*Ziqiang Wang,Changcheng Huang,Chung-Horng Lung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出NEURON-Fabric，一种基于CXL内存控制器的架构，支持低比特梯度聚合（G-Binary和G-Ternary）与FP32路径的动态选择。实验表明该方案开销极低（最多1.67%运行时开销），可大幅压缩梯度通信量至FP32基线的3.6-5.4%，同时通过层感知策略在CIFAR-100/ResNet-18上恢复精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模模型分布式训练中，梯度All-Reduce操作对内存和通信路径的压力日益增大，亟需在保持训练精度的前提下减少梯度通信量。

Method: 设计CXL端控制器架构，支持两种低比特聚合模式：G-Binary（符号计数聚合）和G-Ternary（门控聚合），并提供控制接口实现低比特与FP32路径的动态选择。采用层感知准入机制，对敏感层（如分类器头部）保留FP32路径。

Result: 周期级时序显示低比特聚合数据路径最多增加1.67%运行时开销；训练验证表明全路径低比特聚合在CIFAR-100/ResNet-18上失效，但层感知策略（头部FP32+主干低比特）恢复精度，梯度流量降至FP32基线的3.6-5.4%；硬件综合显示512位聚合路径可作为近内存扩展而非独立加速器。

Conclusion: NEURON-Fabric通过CXL近内存低比特聚合，在极低开销下大幅压缩梯度通信量，并通过细粒度路径控制平衡精度与效率，适合作为分布式训练的内存层次优化方案。

Abstract: In large-model distributed training, especially large language model workloads, gradient All-Reduce increasingly stresses the memory and communication path. This paper asks whether a Compute Express Link (CXL) memory controller can aggregate low-bit gradient signals as gradient cache lines pass through it, while preserving a 32-bit floating-point (FP32) path for workloads, layers, or phases that should not use low-bit approximation. We present NEURON-Fabric, a CXL-side controller architecture that performs packed gradient-binary (G-Binary) sign-count aggregation and gradient-ternary (G-Ternary) gated aggregation near CXL memory, with a control interface for selecting low-bit or FP32 paths. Cycle-level timing experiments show that the measured five-cycle low-bit aggregation datapath adds at most 1.67 percent exposed runtime overhead in the full last-level-cache miss regime; under bandwidth pressure, the same compute stage is hidden by CXL service time. Functional tests confirm byte-exact identity read-back, G-Binary sign-count aggregation, and G-Ternary gating. Training checks quantify the communication and accuracy tradeoff: low-bit aggregation remains close to FP32 on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 and SST-2/DistilBERT, while full-path low-bit aggregation fails on CIFAR-100/ResNet-18. Layer-aware admission identifies the classifier head as sensitive; keeping the head on FP32 while applying low-bit aggregation to the backbone recovers most accuracy and reduces gradient traffic to 3.6-5.4 percent of the FP32 baseline. Hardware synthesis and FPGA place-and-route estimates suggest that the 512-bit aggregation datapath is small enough to be treated as a near-memory datapath extension rather than a separate accelerator-scale block.

</details>


### [207] [Solyx AI Grid: Hardware-Telemetry-Aware Routing Across Geographically Distributed GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15050)
*Aleks Bernhard,Nithin Katla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Solyx AI Grid 是一个跨站点推理路由控制平面，通过整合 GPU 硬件遥测（DCGM）、vLLM 应用指标和广域网实时信号（RTT、抖动）等10个信号的加权压力评分器，实现每请求粒度的放置决策。在跨越三个美国数据中心、八种工作负载类别和216个SLO矩阵的实验中，相比轮询调度，SLO吞吐量提升1.56-1.75倍，能力不匹配泄漏降至0.43%（标准路由器为32%），故障绕行p99延迟为1,247 ms（对比4,226 ms）。GPU硬件遥测平均比应用层SLO违规提前11.2秒预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU算力在地理分布站点间碎片化，导致单集群LLM推理路由假设在跨站点场景下不再成立，亟需一种能够整合多维度信号（硬件、应用、网络）的跨站点推理路由方案。

Method: 提出 Solyx AI Grid 控制平面，集成10个信号（GPU硬件遥测DCGM、vLLM应用指标、WAN实时信号如RTT和抖动）的加权压力评分器，对每个推理请求进行跨站点的最优放置决策。

Result: 在6块H100/H200 SXM GPU和9块RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell SE GPU、三个美国数据中心、八种工作负载、216个SLO矩阵的实验中：相比轮询调度，Tier-2 SLO吞吐量提升1.56-1.75倍；能力不匹配泄漏降至0.43%（对比32%）；故障绕行p99为1,247 ms（对比4,226 ms）；GPU硬件遥测平均提前11.2秒预警SLO违规。

Conclusion: Solyx AI Grid 有效实现了跨站点的实时推理路由优化，且这是首个公开的将硬件遥测、应用指标和活跃WAN路径信号结合的物理多站点LLM推理路由实证研究。

Abstract: As GPU capacity fragments across geographically distributed sites, single-cluster LLM inference routing assumptions break down in measurable ways. We present Solyx AI Grid, a cross-site inference routing control plane that integrates GPU hardware telemetry (DCGM), vLLM application metrics, and real-time WAN signals (RTT, jitter) into per-request placement decisions via a 10-signal weighted pressure scorer. Across two empirical campaigns--six H100/H200 SXM GPUs and nine RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell SE GPUs spanning three US datacenters, eight workload classes, and a 216-cell SLO matrix--Solyx AI Grid delivers 1.56--1.75x throughput at tier-2 SLO over round-robin across all eight classes, cuts capability-mismatch leakage to 0.43% (versus 32% for standard routers), and reroutes around failures at a p99 of 1,247 ms versus 4,226 ms. We further find that GPU hardware telemetry leads application-layer SLO breach by 11.2 seconds on average, enabling proactive traffic drain before user-facing latency impact. To our knowledge, this is the first public empirical study of live physical multi-site LLM inference routing combining hardware telemetry, application metrics, and active WAN path signals.

</details>


### [208] [A RAG-Enhanced Bi-Level Cognitive Orchestration Framework for LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15076)
*Yuhong Jiang,Zhishu Shen,Tong Yin,Qiushi Zheng,Yichao Jin,Fidan Mehmeti,Jiong Jin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对低轨卫星网络中时空资源碎片化问题，提出RAG增强的双层认知编排框架：上层LLM利用离线专家知识库动态推断偏好权重，下层遗传调度器生成物理可行的路由与任务卸载方案，在Walker-Delta网络测试床下实现丢包率降低30.7%、能效提升30%、端到端延迟降低8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络下行链路容量有限，而星上边缘计算虽能缓解压力却引入时空资源碎片化问题——星上计算能力、能量和链路可用性高度动态不均，导致传统静态路由与调度方法性能退化。

Method: 提出RAG增强的双层认知编排框架：上层策略层中LLM通过离线蒸馏的专家知识库，基于实时网络状态紧凑抽象描述动态推断偏好权重；下层执行层中保真度感知的遗传调度器利用该权重计算物理可行、无碰撞的联合路由与任务卸载调度方案。

Result: 在高保真Walker-Delta网络测试床混合关键性负载下，相比最先进学习基线，丢包率降低30.7%，能效提升30%，端到端延迟降低8.5%，且在级联节点故障场景下保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 提出的RAG增强双层认知编排框架有效整合了碎片化资源，在多项指标上显著优于现有方法，并展现出对动态网络故障的强鲁棒性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of remote sensing data in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks is increasingly constrained by limited downlink capacity to terrestrial networks. Satellite edge computing alleviates this pressure by enabling in-orbit data processing. However, it introduces a new challenge of spatio-temporal resource fragmentation. Variations in onboard computing capability, constrained energy availability, and intermittent inter-satellite and satellite-ground connectivity lead to highly dynamic and uneven resource distribution, which degrades the performance of conventional static routing and scheduling approaches. To address this, we propose a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-enhanced bi-level cognitive orchestration framework for knowledge-guided, multi-objective scheduling. The proposed framework explicitly decouples network control across two different operational scales: at the strategic upper level, a Large Language Model (LLM) leverages an offline-distilled Expert Knowledge Base (EKB) to dynamically infer preference weights based on a compact abstract-state descriptor of real-time network conditions. At the lower execution level, a fidelity-aware genetic scheduler utilizes these inferred weights to compute physically feasible, collision-free joint routing and task offloading schedules. Extensive evaluations on a high-fidelity Walker-Delta network testbed under mixed-criticality workloads demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively consolidates fragmented resources, achieving a 30.7% reduction in packet loss, a 30% improvement in energy efficiency over the most competitive learning-based baseline, and an 8.5% decrease in end-to-end latency, while maintaining robust performance under cascading node-failure scenarios.

</details>


### [209] [Coordinated Scheduling for MoE LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15177)
*Yifan Sun,Zhexiang Zhang,Jiantong Jiang,Gholamreza Haffari,Minxian Xu,Feng Liu,Rajkumar Buyya,Adel N. Toosi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Gimbal是一种面向MoE大语言模型的跨层协调调度系统，通过细粒度DP引擎调度器（利用KV缓存、预填负载、队列压力和专家压力等在线后端信号）和扩展的专家负载均衡（结合源DP到专家路由统计及MINLP启发式放置策略），相比vLLM将TTFT降低42.9%，TPOT降低33.3%，高负载吞吐量提升3.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE-LLM服务系统中，前端调度器与后端专家负载均衡器相互隔离，前者仅使用粗略请求计数器进行路由，后者主要依赖聚合专家激活计数，导致系统无法对细粒度引擎压力、后端MoE压力和源相关专家流量做出反应，造成DP引擎失衡和专家级热点。

Method: 1) 细粒度DP引擎调度器：利用KV缓存使用率、预填剩余工作量、队列压力和MoE专家压力等在线后端信号，将请求调度远离过载引擎；内部采用预填感知的队列排序策略（带老化机制）减少队头阻塞，无需输出长度预测。2) 扩展专家负载均衡：结合在线源DP到专家路由统计，使用MINLP启发式方法协同考虑专家负载、源感知通信和迁移稳定性来进行专家放置。

Result: 相比SOTA系统vLLM：平均TTFT降低42.9%，平均TPOT降低33.3%，高负载请求吞吐量提升3.0%。

Conclusion: Gimbal通过跨层协调调度有效解决了MoE服务中DP引擎不平衡和专家热点问题，证明了利用细粒度后端信号和源感知路由统计进行联合调度优化的有效性。

Abstract: Serving Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models (LLMs) is challenging because dynamic request workloads interact with sparse expert routing, creating both data-parallel (DP) engine imbalance and expert-level hotspots. Existing LLM serving systems typically make these decisions in isolation: frontend schedulers route requests using coarse request counters, while backend expert balancers rely mainly on aggregate expert activation counts. This separation prevents the serving system from reacting to fine-grained engine pressure, backend MoE pressure, and source-dependent expert traffic. To address this gap, we propose Gimbal, a coordinated cross-level scheduling system for efficient MoE-based LLM serving. First, Gimbal presents a fine-grained DP-engine scheduler that uses online backend pressure signals, including key-value (KV) cache usage, remaining prefill work, queue pressure, and MoE expert pressure, to dispatch requests away from overloaded engines. Inside each engine, Gimbal further applies a lightweight prefill-aware queue ordering policy with aging to reduce head-of-line blocking without output-length prediction. Second, Gimbal extends expert load balancing with online source-DP-to-expert routing statistics and uses a heuristic guided by a mixed-integer nonlinear program (MINLP) to place experts while jointly considering expert load, source-aware communication, and migration stability. Our evaluation shows that Gimbal reduces average Time To First Token (TTFT) by 42.9% and average Time Per Output Token (TPOT) by 33.3% compared with the state-of-the-art serving system vLLM, while improving high-load request throughput by 3.0%.

</details>


### [210] [Generation Quality-Latency Tradeoff-Aware Inference Offloading for Multimodal LLMs in Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15210)
*Zhongxiao Wang,Yueshen Xu,Xinkui Zhao,Wei Shao,Rui Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对云边协同多模态大模型（MLLM）推理中的内容生成质量与响应延迟联合优化问题，提出QLMIO卸载框架，并构建了MIOBench基准数据集。实验表明该框架相比基线最高可降低58.14%延迟，同时保持与纯云执行相当的任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云边协同部署MLLM的场景下，用户通常关心多个QoS属性（如生成质量和响应延迟），但联合优化这些属性非常困难，现有研究对此问题缺乏有效方案。

Method: 提出QLMIO（Quality-Latency Tradeoff-Aware MLLM Inference Offloading）框架，用于在云边协同MLLM系统中做出最优卸载决策，实现生成质量与响应延迟之间的平衡。同时构建了真实云边协同MLLM系统并收集MIOBench基准数据集以支持评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，QLMIO框架相比基线最高降低58.14%的延迟，同时匹配了纯云执行全部请求时的任务完成率。

Conclusion: QLMIO框架能有效实现云边协同MLLM推理中生成质量与响应延迟的联合优化，为多模态LLM在云边连续体上的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Beyond pure cloud, some efforts are being made to deploy Large Language Models (LLMs) in edge to accelerate inference response. So the deployment of LLMs in cloud-edge continuum becomes a promising paradigm, where the tasks involving multimodal data occupy a large part of requests. Under this continuum, users usually concern about multiple Quality-of-Service (QoS) attributes, but it is always intractable to jointly optimize them. In this paper, we propose to study the joint optimization of those attributes and focus on two key representatives, i.e., content generation quality and response latency. We propose to study the offloading technology to achieve a tradeoff between the two objectives in the cloud-edge collaborative Multimodal LLM (MLLM) system. However, it is highly difficult to predict generation quality and inference latency for MLLM inference tasks while optimizing this offloading process. To address these unprecedented difficulties, we propose a Quality-Latency Tradeoff-Aware MLLM Inference Offloading (QLMIO) framework to make decisions that optimally balance generation quality and response latency. Meanwhile, recognizing the absence of publicly available datasets tailored to the MLLM inference offloading problem, we constructed a real-world cloud-edge collaborative MLLM system and subsequently collected an MLLM Inference Offloading Benchmark (MIOBench) to comprehensively evaluate our framework and facilitate the study of this problem. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the QLMIO framework reduces latency by up to 58.14\% compared to baselines, while simultaneously matching the task completion rate achieved under the case that executes all requests exclusively on a cloud server. The dataset and codes are available at Github.

</details>


### [211] [Adaptive Resource Management and Quality Control for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15319)
*Yifei Xia,Hao Yuan,Suhan Ling,Haoran Sun,Hanke Zhang,Xupeng Miao,Fangcheng Fu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SlackServe，一个基于播放余量的实时视频生成服务系统。它利用播放余量作为统一信号，通过三级优先级队列、重新归巢和弹性序列并行性在流之间重新分配资源，同时通过双模态帕累托路由在质量下限下选择每块 fidelity 配置。在16-H100 GPU集群上，SlackServe将连续播放比(CPR)提升1.64倍-3.29倍，将首块到达时间(TTFC)降低1.61倍-9.65倍，同时保持可比的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归扩散变换器(AR-DiTs)将视频生成从离线范式转变为实时流式范式，但现有服务系统未考虑这种新范式的独特服务级目标(SLO)：保持播放连续性，即生成必须领先于播放时间线。一旦生成落后，用户会经历可见的卡顿。

Method: 提出SlackServe系统，以播放余量(playout slack)作为统一信号驱动两种关键设计：(1) 跨流资源重分配，通过三级优先级队列、重新归巢和弹性序列并行性实现；(2) 流内每块fidelity配置自适应，通过双模态帕累托路由(Bi-Modal Pareto Routing)在质量下限下选择最优配置。

Result: 在16-H100 GPU集群上，SlackServe将QoE指标CPR提升1.64倍-3.29倍，TTFC降低1.61倍-9.65倍，同时生成质量与基线相当。

Conclusion: 实时视频生成具有随播放进度演变的动态SLO，因此资源应优先分配给播放余量较低的流，且按时交付的可接受块优于延迟交付的高保真块。SlackServe通过播放余量驱动的服务系统有效保障了实时流式视频生成的播放连续性。

Abstract: Autoregressive diffusion transformers (AR-DiTs) recast video generation from an offline paradigm to a real-time streaming one: the model generates video one chunk at a time, making each chunk available for playout once produced. The service-level objective (SLO) for this paradigm is no longer fixed latency or throughput but the preservation of playout continuity: generation must stay ahead of the playout timeline. Once generation falls behind, the remaining playable buffer (playout slack) is exhausted, and users experience visible stalls. This objective reveals two serving design insights. First, real-time video generation has a dynamic SLO that evolves with playout progress, so resources should move toward streams with lower playout slack. Second, an acceptable chunk delivered on time is preferable to a late high-fidelity chunk, so per-chunk fidelity configurations should adapt to available playout slack. Guided by these insights, we present SlackServe, a playout-slack-driven serving system that preserves playout continuity in real-time streaming video generation. SlackServe uses playout slack as a unified signal, reallocating resources across streams through three-tier priority queues, re-homing, and elastic sequence parallelism, while selecting per-chunk fidelity configurations within each stream through Bi-Modal Pareto Routing under a quality floor. On a 16-H100 GPU cluster, SlackServe improves Quality of Experience (QoE), measured by Continuous Play Ratio (CPR), by 1.64x-3.29x and reduces Time to First Chunk (TTFC) by 1.61x-9.65x over baselines, while preserving comparable generation quality.

</details>


### [212] [CoAgent: Concurrency Control for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15376)
*Hongtao Lyu,Dingyan Zhang,Mingyu Wu,Xingda Wei,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MTPO协议和CoAgent系统，通过让LLM智能体自我判断冲突并修复计划，而非采用传统锁或乐观并发控制，在保持可串行化的同时实现近线性的并发加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在共享状态上并行运行时存在写-写冲突，传统并发控制机制（2PL、OCC）因智能体推理时间长、读集宽泛不透明、状态实时生效等特点而效率极低，需要一种适配LLM特性的新机制。

Method: 提出MTPO（单调轨迹预序）协议：启动时固定序列化顺序，读取按序过滤后的值，写操作推测性原地执行；通过单向通知告知受影响读取者重新判断和修复计划，框架通过预注册的saga式逆操作机械性地撤销和重排错位的写操作。

Result: 在十个带冲突的工作负载上，CoAgent保持5%以内的串行正确性，获得1.4倍加速和近串行的token成本；在bash目标系统上，在线生长25个工具的库，任务通过率从45/71提升至63/71，时间和成本分别降至0.80倍和0.86倍。

Conclusion: 利用LLM智能体的语义理解能力实现告知性并发控制（而非强制性控制），在保持正确性的同时大幅提升并发效率，展示了超越经典事务模型的可行性。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems -- coding agents, devops agents, document agents -- now routinely run several agents in parallel against the same git tree, Kubernetes cluster, or document. As soon as two of them mutate shared state, they enter the regime classical concurrency control has studied for decades, but classical mechanisms fit LLM agents poorly. A single agent transaction spans minutes of inference, read sets are broad and opaque rather than statically inferable, and the live state agents act on admits neither fork nor buffer, so writes take effect the moment they execute. Locks block long inference intervals; OCC abort-and-retry discards minutes of work on every conflict.
  This paper builds concurrency control on a capability classical transactions lack: the LLM inside each agent can judge whether a conflicting write invalidates its plan, and can repair exactly the operations that depended on it. Control therefore turns advisory: the runtime informs, the agent repairs. Our protocol, MTPO (Monotonic Trajectory Pre-Order), fixes a serialization order at launch, serves each read the order-filtered value, and applies writes speculatively in place; a one-way notification asks an affected reader to re-judge and patch its plan, while the framework mechanically undoes and reorders misplaced writes through the saga-style inverse each tool registers in advance. At quiescence the run is serializable in the pre-decided order. We realize MTPO as CoAgent, toolcall middleware whose privileged ToolSmith grows footprint-declared, undoable tools online. On ten contended workloads, CoAgent stays within 5\% of serial correctness at a $1.4\times$ speedup and near-serial token cost, where 2PL and OCC surrender nearly all concurrency gains; on a bash-only target system, it grows a 25-tool library online and lifts the task pass rate from 45/71 to 63/71 at $0.80\times$ the time and $0.86\times$ the cost.

</details>


### [213] [Is RISC-V Ready for Massively Parallel Astrophysical Codes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15490)
*Jenny Lynn Almerol,Nitin Shukla,Federico Ficarelli,Geray S. Karademir,Andrea Bartolini,Emanuele Venieri,Giacomo Madella,Elisabetta Boella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了三个天体物理生产代码（iPIC3D、PLUTO、OpenGGCM）在RISC-V（Sophgo SG2044）、x86（AMD EPYC 9554）和ARM（NVIDIA GH200 Grace）平台上的性能与可移植性。RISC-V相对x86慢约3-6倍，相对ARM慢约5-9倍，主要瓶颈在于内存带宽、共享缓存、128位向量单元和编译器自动向量化能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估新兴RISC-V架构在科学计算领域（特别是天体物理生产代码）的实际性能表现和可移植性，量化其与成熟架构（x86、ARM）的性能差距，识别关键瓶颈。

Method: 选取三种代表不同计算特征的天体物理生产代码（iPIC3D内存密集、PLUTO计算密集、OpenGGCM混合型），分别在RISC-V (Sophgo SG2044)、x86 (AMD EPYC 9554)和ARM (NVIDIA GH200 Grace)平台上运行，验证数值正确性，对比性能表现，分析MPI+OpenMP混合并行配置。

Result: RISC-V性能显著低于x86（慢约3-6倍）和ARM（慢约5-9倍）。内存密集型内核受影响最大，原因包括内存带宽饱和、L2缓存竞争、128位向量单元限制、编译器自动向量化能力弱。混合MPI+OpenMP配置存在内存竞争与通信开销之间的权衡。

Conclusion: RISC-V能够支持科学工作负载，但硬件（内存带宽、缓存、向量单元）和编译器技术（尤其是自动向量化）需要进一步改进才能达到具有竞争力的性能水平。

Abstract: We present a performance and portability evaluation of three well-established astrophysical production codes, namely iPIC3D, PLUTO, and OpenGGCM, on a Sophgo SG2044 RISC-V processor (part of the Monte Cimone cluster), with comparisons to AMD EPYC 9554 (x86) and NVIDIA GH200 Grace (ARM) systems. These applications represent memory-bound, compute-bound, and hybrid workloads, respectively. Numerical correctness is verified across all platforms, confirming portability. RISC-V shows consistently lower performance, with slowdowns of about $3-6\times$ relative to x86 and $5-9\times$ relative to ARM. The gap is mainly due to limited memory bandwidth, shared cache constraints, narrower 128-bit vector units, and lower clock frequency, but also less-mature auto-vectorization capability of the GNU compiler suite. Memory-bound kernels are the most affected, where early bandwidth saturation and L2 cache contention reduce scalability at higher thread counts. Hybrid MPI+OpenMP configurations reveal a trade-off between memory contention and communication overhead, with intermediate configurations achieving the best performance. These results suggest that RISC-V is capable of supporting scientific workloads; however, additional improvements in both hardware and compiler technology, particularly in auto-vectorization, are required to achieve competitive performance.

</details>


### [214] [Quantifying the Impact of Lossy Compression on Neural Generative Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15959)
*Zhimin Li,Harshitha Menon,Charles Jekel,Valerio Pascucci,Peter Lindstrom*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了训练数据的**有损压缩**对生成式代理模型质量的影响，通过利用神经网络训练中的固有变异性，提出了一种估算模型可容忍压缩误差的方法。实验表明，该方法可节省高达39倍的数据存储，并加速训练3倍，而对模型质量影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真的生成式代理模型需要大规模训练数据集，带来了存储和I/O挑战。有损压缩虽能减轻负担，但压缩误差对模型质量的影响难以量化。

Method: 首先刻画神经网络训练中的固有不确定性，证明相同训练配置会产生不同模型。利用这种变异性，提出一种估算代理模型能容忍多少压缩误差而不影响精度的方法。

Result: 在两个应用仿真上，有损压缩节省了高达23.7倍和39倍的数据存储，对代理模型质量影响可忽略不计；同时数据加载速度提升，训练时间减少高达3倍。

Conclusion: 有损压缩能显著降低存储需求并加速训练，同时保持代理模型的高质量，是一种有效的训练数据压缩方案。

Abstract: Neural networks are used as generative surrogate models for scientific discovery, which are trainable approximations of scientific simulations. These models enable users to replace time-consuming numerical simulations with learned alternatives, providing quick solutions. However, high-fidelity generative surrogate models require massive training datasets, which can create storage and I/O challenges. Lossy compression is a promising way to reduce this burden, but compression errors may affect the model quality in subtle ways, making it challenging to quantify their impact.
  In this work, we examine how lossy compression of training data impacts the quality of generative surrogate models. We begin by characterizing the uncertainty inherent in training neural networks, showing that identical training configurations can produce different models. By exploiting this variability, we propose a method to estimate how much compression-induced error a surrogate model can tolerate without affecting its accuracy. Evaluation of two application simulations demonstrates that our approach significantly reduces memory/storage requirements and speeds up training while producing high-quality surrogate models. These results show that lossy compression saves data storage up to 23.7x and 39x with negligible impact on the quality of the surrogate model. Meanwhile, reducing the size of the training data set also enhances the data loading speed and reduces the training time by up to 3x.

</details>


### [215] [PreLort: Prefix-Nested LoRA for Federated Fine-Tuning under Rank Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15963)
*Muhammad Waseem,Nurbek Tastan,Andrej Jovanovic,Nicholas D. Lane,Nils Lukas,Karthik Nandakumar,Samuel Horvath*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出PreLort，一种基于嵌套低秩结构的异构联邦LoRA方法，通过前缀层级组织适配器维度，配合分段聚合和前缀嵌套训练策略，在不同秩客户端间实现任务相关信息的高效共享，在多项指标上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构联邦LoRA方法在聚合时无法控制信息在不同秩维度上的分布，导致低秩共享表示利用不充分，限制了异构客户端间的协作效果。

Method: 1) 嵌套低秩结构：将适配器维度组织成前缀层级，低秩维度编码任务相关信息，高秩维度捕获额外容量；2) 分段聚合规则：仅对贡献到每个秩分段的客户端进行平均，避免低秩客户端的零填充稀释；3) 前缀嵌套训练策略：在多种秩截断下优化每个适配器，促使有用信号集中到低秩前缀维度。

Result: 在准确率、ROUGE-L上一致优于先前异构联邦LoRA方法，困惑度更低或相当，跨多个基座模型验证有效。

Conclusion: PreLort通过结构化的秩层级设计和针对性训练-聚合策略，有效提升了异构联邦LoRA中低秩客户端对高秩客户端丰富信息的利用能力，实现了更优的联邦微调效果。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models using parameter-efficient methods such as LoRA enables privacy-preserving adaptation of foundation models. Heterogeneous hardware resources introduce challenges, as clients with different adapter ranks cannot be directly aggregated. While existing methods enable aggregation under heterogeneous ranks, they fail to control how information is distributed across rank dimensions, leading to suboptimal use of shared low-rank representations. Instead, we propose PreLort: a nested low-rank formulation for federated LoRA that organizes adapter dimensions into a prefix hierarchy. Our approach ensures that lower-rank dimensions encode task-relevant information, while higher-rank dimensions capture additional capacity. Building on this, we introduce (i) a segment-wise aggregation rule that averages only over clients contributing to each rank segment, avoiding dilution from zero-padded lower-rank clients, and (ii) a prefix-nested training strategy that optimizes each adapter under multiple rank truncations, encouraging useful signal to concentrate in low-rank prefix dimensions. Together, these components encourage a consistent low-rank prefix capturing the most task-relevant information, while higher-rank dimensions learn additional capacity. This allows low-rank clients to benefit from richer information contributed by higher-rank clients, as prefix dimensions are consistently learned and aggregated. Experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms prior heterogeneous federated LoRA methods in accuracy and ROUGE-L, while achieving lower or comparable perplexity across multiple base models.

</details>


### [216] [Raiders of the Lost Log: Synchronous Parallel In-Place Models and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15969)
*Michael T. Goodrich,Vinesh Sridhar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出同步PIP模型族，解决异步PIP模型在中间处理器数量下无原地保证的问题，并通过并行增强扫描技术确保算法在所有处理器数量下保持高效和严格原地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIP模型假设异步处理，仅在极限高处理器数量或顺序执行时有原地保证，缺乏对中间处理器数量的原地保证，存在理论空白。

Method: 提出Synchronous PIP模型族，设计高效同步原地并行算法（零辅助共享内存，每处理器仅需常数额外私有内存），并引入并行增强扫描技术来保证所有处理器数量下的严格原地特性。

Result: 在Synchronous PIP模型下给出了无需辅助共享内存、仅需常数额外私有内存的高效同步原地并行算法。

Conclusion: 新提出的同步PIP模型填补了原地并行计算在中间处理器数量下的理论空白，并行增强扫描技术确保了算法在任意处理器规模下的原地性和效率。

Abstract: Embedded systems and Internet of Things (IoT) applications motivate in-place parallel algorithms, which avoid allocating additional shared memory past the input. Work by Gu, Obeya, and Shun [APOCS '21] defines a family of PIP (parallel in-place) models and parallel algorithms that eschew auxiliary memory at high processor counts while remaining in-situ when run sequentially. However, their models assume asynchronous processing and have no in-place guarantees for intermediate processor counts. We address this gap in the literature by proposing a Synchronous PIP family of models for in-place parallel and distributed computation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our new model by giving efficient and synchronous parallel algorithms in this model that require no auxiliary shared memory and only constant private memory per processor. Importantly, we show how to leverage a new parallel-augmented sweep technique to ensure that Synchronous PIP algorithms remain efficient and strictly in-place at all processor counts.

</details>


### [217] [SwiftCache: Efficient LLM Serving for Multi-turn Conversations with Heterogeneous KV Cache Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16135)
*Jianmin Hu,Minxian Xu,Sa Wang,Chong Ma,Min Shen,Kejiang Ye,Lin Qu,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SwiftCache提出了一种协作推理系统，通过异构模型间共享GPU显存和NVLink带宽，将低KV缓存需求模型的空闲显存捐赠给高需求模型存储前缀缓存，并仅在本地GPU保留当前活跃层的KV缓存，从而减少PCIe传输、降低TTFT延迟并延长上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中历史token持续累积，现有系统虽缓存KV对以避免重复计算，但GPU显存（HBM）容量有限导致KV缓存被迫卸载到CPU内存或SSD，使得上下文增长时KV缓存重载延迟显著增加；同时HBM容量限制也制约了最大推理长度，进而限制对话支持轮数。

Method: SwiftCache构建协作推理系统，使服务器内异构模型共享闲置GPU显存和NVLink带宽：低KV缓存需求模型将空闲显存捐赠用于存储高需求模型的前缀缓存，通过NVLink实现跨模型KV缓存共享以避免慢速PCIe传输；同时仅将当前活跃层的KV缓存保留在本地GPU显存中，减少内存压力以支持更长上下文推理。

Result: 在真实负载实验中，相比vLLM和SGLang，SwiftCache将P99首次token生成时间（TTFT）降低最多69%，最大上下文长度提升最多3.98倍，且对同驻模型的干扰极小。

Conclusion: SwiftCache通过异构模型间显存协作和NVLink共享，有效缓解了KV缓存卸载带来的延迟瓶颈和显存容量限制，显著提升多轮对话场景下的推理效率与上下文长度。

Abstract: Multi-turn conversation is a fundamental scenario in LLM applications, widely used in chatbots and AI agents. As the conversation evolves, historical tokens accumulate continuously. Existing systems cache their key-value (KV) pairs to avoid redundant computation. However, limited GPU memory (HBM) capacity often forces these KV caches to be offloaded to CPU memory or SSD, making KV cache reloads increasingly costly in terms of latency as the context grows. Meanwhile, the constrained HBM capacity also limits the maximum inference length, thereby restricting the number of turns that can be supported in a conversation.
  To address these two challenges, we propose SwiftCache, a collaborative inference system that enables heterogeneous models to share underutilized GPU memory and NVLink bandwidth within a server. Specifically, models with low KV cache demand donate idle GPU memory to store the prefix cache of high-demand models, allowing cross-model KV cache sharing over NVLink and avoiding slow PCIe transfers. SwiftCache further reduces memory pressure by keeping only the KV cache of the currently active layer in local GPU memory, thereby enabling longer-context inference. Our experiments on real-world workloads show that SwiftCache reduces P99 time-to-first-token (TTFT) by up to 69% and extends maximum context length by up to 3.98x compared to vLLM and SGLang, with minimal interference to co-located models.

</details>


### [218] [Efficient Data Availability Sampling via Coded Distributed Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16200)
*Dang Pham Minh,Hung Vuong Huu,Duc A. Tran*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CDA提出了一种基于编码分布式数组的数据可用性采样新设计，利用网络编码提升鲁棒性和效率，相比以太坊最新的RDA方案性能提升数倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链网络中的全复制模型无法随区块大小扩展，导致区块传播慢、数据重复传输、共识达成时间长。尽管以太坊采用DAS方案，但现有工作聚焦于密码学基础，而P2P网络层的拜占庭容错和可扩展性机制尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出CDA（Coded Distributed Arrays）设计，基于编码分布式数组，利用网络编码实现数据可用性采样中的片段的发现与路由，兼顾拜占庭容错和可扩展性。

Result: 评估实验对比CDA与以太坊最新DAS方案RDA，CDA在多个指标上实现了数倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: CDA通过编码分布式数组和网络编码，有效解决了DAS中P2P网络层的扩展性和鲁棒性问题，显著优于现有方案。

Abstract: Data availability is a fundamental bottleneck in modern blockchain networks.
